24. On Thursday, FOX NEWS aired two debates: one in the afternoon of the lower tiered nationally polled candidates and an evening 

debate of the top ten Republican candidates. Did you watch both debates, just the evening debate, just the afternoon debate, or 

did you not watch either debate?
